func _build_chapter_select() -> void:

	chapter_select_layer.name = "ChapterSelect"
	add_child(chapter_select_layer)

	var background := ColorRect.new()
	background.color = Color(0.03, 0.07, 0.06)

func _create_grid(width: int, height: int) -> Array:
	var grid := []
	for _y in range(height):
		var row := []
		for _x in range(width):
			row.append(WALL)
		grid.append(row)
	return grid


func _carve_room(grid: Array, room: Rect2i) -> void:
	for y in range(room.position.y, room.end.y):
		for x in range(room.position.x, room.end.x):
			grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _connect_rooms(grid: Array, from_room: Rect2i, to_room: Rect2i) -> void:
	var a := _room_center_cell(from_room)
	var b := _room_center_cell(to_room)
	for x in range(mini(a.x, b.x), maxi(a.x, b.x) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(x, a.y))
	for y in range(mini(a.y, b.y), maxi(a.y, b.y) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(b.x, y))


func _carve_corridor_cell(grid: Array, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	for y in range(cell.y - 1, cell.y + 2):
		for x in range(cell.x - 1, cell.x + 2):
			if y >= 0 and y < grid.size() and x >= 0 and x < grid[y].size():
				grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _add_tile(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i, atlas_cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = dungeon_tileset
	sprite.region_enabled = true
	sprite.region_rect = _dungeon_rect(atlas_cell)
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	sprite.position = _cell_center(cell)
	parent.add_child(sprite)


func _visual_tile_for_cell(cell: Vector2i, is_wall: bool) -> Vector2i:
	if is_wall:
		return WALL_ALT_TILE if (cell.x + cell.y) % 3 == 0 else WALL_TILE
	return FLOOR_ALT_TILE if (cell.x + cell.y) % 4 == 0 else FLOOR_TILE


func _add_wall_collision(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.position = _cell_center(cell)
	body.collision_layer = LAYER_WORLD
	body.collision_mask = 0
	parent.add_child(body)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var shape := RectangleShape2D.new()
	shape.size = Vector2(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE)
	collision.shape = shape
	body.add_child(collision)

func _facing_vector() -> Vector2:
	match player_facing:
		"left":
			return Vector2.LEFT
		"right":
			return Vector2.RIGHT
		"up":
			return Vector2.UP
		_:
			return Vector2.DOWN


func _build_camera() -> void:
	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "Camera"
	camera.enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 8.0
	camera.zoom = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = MAP_GRID_WIDTH * TILE_SIZE
	camera.limit_bottom = MAP_GRID_HEIGHT * TILE_SIZE
	player.add_child(camera)

func _on_collectible_body_entered(body: Node, area: Area2D) -> void:
	if body != player or finished:
		return
	if bool(area.get_meta("opened")):
		return

	var kind := String(area.get_meta("kind"))
	var amount := int(area.get_meta("amount"))
	area.set_meta("opened", true)
	var sprite := area.get_node_or_null("ChestSprite") as Sprite2D
	if sprite:
		sprite.texture = _open_chest_texture()
	var collision := area.get_node_or_null("ChestCollision") as CollisionShape2D
	if collision:
		collision.set_deferred("disabled", true)

	if kind == "gem":
		collected_gems += amount

	_update_hud()

	return chest_open

func _start_player_down() -> void:

func _update_room_discovery() -> void:
	for index in range(rooms.size()):
		if rooms[index].has_point(Vector2i(floori(player.position.x / TILE_SIZE), floori(player.position.y / TILE_SIZE))):

func _finish_exploration() -> void:
	finished = true
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	summary_panel.visible = true
	retry_button.visible = false
	message_label.text = ""
	summary_label.text = "Chapter Complete\n\nChapter: %d / %d\nLevel: %d / %d\nCoins: %d / %d\nGems: %d / %d\nRooms: %d / %d\n\nPress Enter to replay this chapter." % [
		current_chapter,
		CHAPTER_COUNT,
		current_level,
		LEVEL_COUNT,
		collected_coins,
		total_coins,
		collected_gems,
		total_gems,
		discovered_rooms.size(),
		ROOM_COUNT,
	]


func _update_hud() -> void:
	if not hud_label:
		return
	hud_label.text = "Chapter %d  Level %d    HP %d / %d    Coins %d / %d    Gems %d / %d    Rooms %d / %d" % [
		current_chapter,
		current_level,
		player_hp,
		PLAYER_MAX_HP,
		collected_coins,
		total_coins,
		collected_gems,
		total_gems,
		discovered_rooms.size(),
		ROOM_COUNT,
	]


func _room_center_cell(room: Rect2i) -> Vector2i:
	return room.position + Vector2i(room.size.x / 2, room.size.y / 2)


func _cell_center(cell: Vector2i) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(cell.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE * 0.5, cell.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE * 0.5)


func _atlas_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * TILE_SOURCE_STRIDE, cell.y * TILE_SOURCE_STRIDE, TILE_SOURCE_SIZE, TILE_SOURCE_SIZE)


func _dungeon_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, cell.y * DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE)


func _icon_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * ICON_TILE_SIZE, cell.y * ICON_TILE_SIZE, ICON_TILE_SIZE, ICON_TILE_SIZE)

func _add_collectibles(room_list: Array[Rect2i]) -> void:
	var collectible_root := Node2D.new()
	collectible_root.name = "Collectibles"
	add_child(collectible_root)

	for room_index in range(room_list.size()):
		var room := room_list[room_index]
		var coin_cells := _collectible_cells_for_room(room, room_index)
		for cell in coin_cells:
			var coin_amount := _reward_amount_for_cell("coin", cell)
			_add_collectible(collectible_root, cell, "coin", coin_amount, room_index > 0)
			total_coins += coin_amount

		if room_index > 0:
			var cell := _room_center_cell(room)
			var gem_amount := _reward_amount_for_cell("gem", cell)
			_add_collectible(collectible_root, cell, "gem", gem_amount, true)
			total_gems += gem_amount


func _collectible_cells_for_room(room: Rect2i, room_index: int) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	var cells: Array[Vector2i] = []
	cells.append(room.position + Vector2i(1, 1))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.end.x - 2, room.position.y + 1))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.position.x + 1, room.end.y - 2))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.end.x - 2, room.end.y - 2))
	if room_index > 0 and room_index % 2 == 0:
		cells.append(_room_center_cell(room) + Vector2i(1, 0))
	return cells


func _reward_amount_for_cell(kind: String, cell: Vector2i) -> int:
	if kind == "gem":
		return 1 + ((cell.x + cell.y) % 2)
	return 6 + ((cell.x * 7 + cell.y * 5) % 9)

	var shape := CircleShape2D.new()
	shape.radius = 22.0
	collision.shape = shape
	area.add_child(collision)
	area.body_entered.connect(_on_collectible_body_entered.bind(area))

	if spawn_guards:

+	var image := Image.create(size, size, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
+	image.fill(Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
+
+	var center := Vector2(size * 0.5, size * 0.5)
+	for y in range(size):
+		for x in range(size):
+			var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+			var offset := point - center
+			var basin := Vector2(offset.x / 22.0, offset.y / 15.0).length()
+			var water := Vector2(offset.x / 16.0, offset.y / 10.0).length()
+			var inner := Vector2(offset.x / 8.0, offset.y / 5.0).length()
+			if basin <= 1.0:
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.28, 0.34, 0.32, 1.0))
+			if water <= 1.0:<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="175" />e)
+	var timer := area.get_node_or_null("HealTimer") as Timer
+	if timer:
+		timer.start()
+
+
+func _on_fountain_body_exited(body: Node, area: Area2D) -> void:
+	if body != player:
+		return
+	var timer := area.get_node_or_null("HealTimer") as Timer
+	if timer:
+		timer.stop()
+
+
+func _on_fountain_timer_timeout(area: Area2D) -> void:
+	if not is_instance_valid(area) or finished:
+		return
+	if area.get_overlapping_bodies().has(player):
+		_try_heal_player_from_fountain(area, false)
+
+
+func _try_heal_player_from_fountain(_area: Area2D, show_full_message: bool) -> void:
+	if player_dead or player_hp >= PLAYER_MAX_HP:
+		if show_full_message and message_label:
+			message_label.text = "Healing fountain: HP is full."
+		return
+
+	var before := player_hp
+	player_hp = mini(PLAYER_MAX_HP, player_hp + FOUNTAIN_HEAL_AMOUNT)
+	var healed := player_hp - before
+	message_label.text = "Healing fountain restored +%d HP." % healed
+	_show_player_heal_popup(healed)
+	_update_hud()
+	_update_stats_panel()
+
+
+func _show_player_heal_popup(amount: int) -> void:
+	var popup := Node2D.new()
+	popup.name = "PlayerHealPopup"
+	popup.position = player.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -42.0)
+	add_child(popup)
+
+	var label := Label.new()
+	label.text = "+%d" % amount
+	label.position = Vector2(-10.0, -12.0)
+	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 18)
+	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 1.0, 0.62))
+	label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.03, 0.12, 0.06))
+	label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 4)
+	popup.add_child(label)
+
+	var tween := create_tween()
+	tween.set_parallel(true)
+	tween.tween_property(popup, "position:y", popup.position.y - 30.0, 0.65)
+	tween.tween_property(popup, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.65).set_delay(0.12)
+	tween.set_parallel(false)
+	tween.tween_callback(popup.queue_free)
+
+
 func _add_exit(room: Rect2i) -> void:
 	exit_area = Area2D.new()
 	exit_area.name = "Exit"
*** End Patch

func _create_grid(width: int, height: int) -> Array:
	var grid := []
	for _y in range(height):
		var row := []
		for _x in range(width):
			row.append(WALL)
		grid.append(row)
	return grid


func _carve_room(grid: Array, room: Rect2i) -> void:
	for y in range(room.position.y, room.end.y):
		for x in range(room.position.x, room.end.x):
			grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _connect_rooms(grid: Array, from_room: Rect2i, to_room: Rect2i) -> void:
	var a := _room_center_cell(from_room)
	var b := _room_center_cell(to_room)
	for x in range(mini(a.x, b.x), maxi(a.x, b.x) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(x, a.y))
	for y in range(mini(a.y, b.y), maxi(a.y, b.y) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(b.x, y))


func _carve_corridor_cell(grid: Array, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	for y in range(cell.y - 1, cell.y + 2):
		for x in range(cell.x - 1, cell.x + 2):
			if y >= 0 and y < grid.size() and x >= 0 and x < grid[y].size():
				grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _add_tile(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i, atlas_cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = dungeon_tileset
	sprite.region_enabled = true
	sprite.region_rect = _dungeon_rect(atlas_cell)
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	sprite.position = _cell_center(cell)
	parent.add_child(sprite)


func _visual_tile_for_cell(cell: Vector2i, is_wall: bool) -> Vector2i:
	if is_wall:
		return WALL_ALT_TILE if (cell.x + cell.y) % 3 == 0 else WALL_TILE
	return FLOOR_ALT_TILE if (cell.x + cell.y) % 4 == 0 else FLOOR_TILE


func _add_wall_collision(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.position = _cell_center(cell)
	body.collision_layer = LAYER_WORLD
	body.collision_mask = 0
	parent.add_child(body)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var shape := RectangleShape2D.new()
	shape.size = Vector2(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE)
	collision.shape = shape
	body.add_child(collision)

func _facing_vector() -> Vector2:
	match player_facing:
		"left":
			return Vector2.LEFT
		"right":
			return Vector2.RIGHT
		"up":
			return Vector2.UP
		_:
			return Vector2.DOWN

	camera.name = "Camera"
	camera.enabled = true

	camera.zoom = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
	camera.limit_left = 0

	camera.limit_right = MAP_GRID_WIDTH * TILE_SIZE
	camera.limit_bottom = MAP_GRID_HEIGHT * TILE_SIZE
	player.add_child(camera)

func _on_collectible_body_entered(body: Node, area: Area2D) -> void:
	if body != player or finished:
		return
	if bool(area.get_meta("opened")):
		return

	var kind := String(area.get_meta("kind"))
	var amount := int(area.get_meta("amount"))
	area.set_meta("opened", true)
	var sprite := area.get_node_or_null("ChestSprite") as Sprite2D
	if sprite:
		sprite.texture = _open_chest_texture()
	var collision := area.get_node_or_null("ChestCollision") as CollisionShape2D
	if collision:
		collision.set_deferred("disabled", true)

	if kind == "gem":
		collected_gems += amount
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d gems." % amount
	else:
		collected_coins += amount
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d coins." % amount
	_show_reward_popup(area.global_position, kind, amount)
	_update_hud()


func _closed_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_closed:
		return forest_chest_closed
	return chest_closed


func _open_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_open:
		return forest_chest_open
	return chest_open


func _toggle_stats_panel() -> void:
	stats_panel.visible = not stats_panel.visible
	if stats_panel.visible:
		_update_stats_panel()


func _show_retry_panel() -> void:
	summary_panel.visible = true
	summary_label.text = "You Collapsed\n\nHP: 0 / %d\nCoins: %d / %d\nGems: %d / %d" % [
		PLAYER_MAX_HP,
		collected_coins,
		total_coins,
		collected_gems,
		total_gems,

func _start_player_hurt() -> void:
	if player_dead:
		return
	player_hurt = true
	player_attacking = false
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	_play_player_animation("hurt_%s" % player_facing)


func _start_player_down() -> void:
	player_dead = true
	player_hurt = false
	player_attacking = false
	finished = true
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	message_label.text = "You collapsed."
	_play_player_animation("downed")
	_show_retry_panel()


func _show_player_damage_popup(amount: int) -> void:
	var popup := Node2D.new()
	popup.name = "PlayerDamagePopup"
	popup.position = player.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -34.0)
	add_child(popup)

	var label := Label.new()
	label.text = "-%d" % amount
	label.position = Vector2(-10.0, -12.0)
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 18)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.3, 0.72, 1.0))
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.02, 0.08, 0.16))
	label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 4)
	popup.add_child(label)

	var tween := create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(popup, "position:y", popup.position.y - 30.0, 0.65)
	tween.tween_property(popup, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.65).set_delay(0.12)
	tween.set_parallel(false)
	tween.tween_callback(popup.queue_free)

func _restart_from_level_one() -> void:

	get_tree().set_meta("level", 1)
	get_tree().reload_current_scene()


func _update_stats_panel() -> void:
	if not stats_label:
		return
	stats_label.text = "Character\n\nHP: %d / %d\nAttack: %d\n\nEquipment\nWeapon: Training Staff (+0 ATK)\nRobe: Traveler Robe (+0 HP)\nCharm: Empty Slot" % [
		player_hp,
		PLAYER_MAX_HP,
		PLAYER_ATTACK_POWER,
	]


func _show_reward_popup(origin: Vector2, kind: String, amount: int) -> void:
	var popup := Node2D.new()
	popup.name = "RewardPopup"
	popup.position = origin + Vector2(0.0, -26.0)
	add_child(popup)

	var icon := Sprite2D.new()
	if kind == "coin":
		icon.texture = coin_icon
	else:
		icon.texture = icon_sheet
		icon.region_enabled = true
		icon.region_rect = _icon_rect(ICON_GEM_TILE)
	icon.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
	icon.position = Vector2(-18.0, 0.0)
	popup.add_child(icon)

	var label := Label.new()
	label.text = "+%d" % amount
	label.position = Vector2(2.0, -13.0)
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 18)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.62) if kind == "coin" else Color(0.65, 0.95, 1.0))
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.08, 0.07, 0.06))
	label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 4)
	popup.add_child(label)

	var tween := create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(popup, "position:y", popup.position.y - 34.0, 0.85).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_CUBIC).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_property(popup, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.85).set_delay(0.15)
	tween.set_parallel(false)
	tween.tween_callback(popup.queue_free)


func _on_exit_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body != player or finished:
		return
	if current_le<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="46" />red(body: Node) -> void:
	if body != player or finished:
		return
	get_tree().set_meta("level", maxi(1, current_level - 1))
	get_tree().reload_current_scene()


func _update_room_discovery() -> void:
	for index in range(rooms.size()):
		if rooms[index].has_point(Vector2i(floori(player.position.x / TILE_SIZE), floori(player.position.y / TILE_SIZE))):
			discovered_rooms[index] = true
			_update_hud()
			return


func _finish_exploration() -> void:
	finished = true
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	summary_panel.visible = true
	retry_button.visible = false
	message_label.text = ""
	summary_label.text = "Chapter Complete\n\nChapter: %d / %d\nLevel: %d / %d\nCoins: %d / %d\nGems: %d / %d\nRooms: %d / %d\n\nPress Enter to replay this chapter." % [
		current_chapter,
		CHAPTER_COUNT,
		current_level,
		LEVEL_COUNT,
		collected_coins,
		total_coins,
		collected_gems,
		total_gems,
		discovered_rooms.size(),
		ROOM_COUNT,
	]


func _update_hud() -> void:
	if not hud_label:
		return
	hud_label.text = "Chapter %d  Level %d    HP %d / %d    Coins %d / %d    Gems %d / %d    Rooms %d / %d" % [
		current_chapter,
		current_level,
		player_hp,
		PLAYER_MAX_HP,
		collected_coins,
		total_coins,
		collected_gems,
		total_gems,
		discovered_rooms.size(),
		ROOM_COUNT,
	]


func _room_center_cell(room: Rect2i) -> Vector2i:
	return room.position + Vector2i(room.size.x / 2, room.size.y / 2)


func _cell_center(cell: Vector2i) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(cell.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE * 0.5, cell.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE * 0.5)


func _atlas_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * TILE_SOURCE_STRIDE, cell.y * TILE_SOURCE_STRIDE, TILE_SOURCE_SIZE, TILE_SOURCE_SIZE)


func _dungeon_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, cell.y * DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE, DUNGEON_TILE_SIZE)


func _icon_rect(cell: Vector2i) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(cell.x * ICON_TILE_SIZE, cell.y * ICON_TILE_SIZE, ICON_TILE_SIZE, ICON_TILE_SIZE)

func _add_collectibles(room_list: Array[Rect2i]) -> void:
	var collectible_root := Node2D.new()
	collectible_root.name = "Collectibles"
	add_child(collectible_root)

	for room_index in range(room_list.size()):
		var room := room_list[room_index]
		var coin_cells := _collectible_cells_for_room(room, room_index)
		for cell in coin_cells:
			var coin_amount := _reward_amount_for_cell("coin", cell)
			_add_collectible(collectible_root, cell, "coin", coin_amount, room_index > 0)
			total_coins += coin_amount

		if room_index > 0:
			var cell := _room_center_cell(room)
			var gem_amount := _reward_amount_for_cell("gem", cell)
			_add_collectible(collectible_root, cell, "gem", gem_amount, true)
			total_gems += gem_amount


func _collectible_cells_for_room(room: Rect2i, room_index: int) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	var cells: Array[Vector2i] = []
	cells.append(room.position + Vector2i(1, 1))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.end.x - 2, room.position.y + 1))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.position.x + 1, room.end.y - 2))
	cells.append(Vector2i(room.end.x - 2, room.end.y - 2))
	if room_index > 0 and room_index % 2 == 0:
		cells.append(_room_center_cell(room) + Vector2i(1, 0))
	return cells


func _reward_amount_for_cell(kind: String, cell: Vector2i) -> int:
	if kind == "gem":
		return 1 + ((cell.x + cell.y) % 2)
	return 6 + ((cell.x * 7 + cell.y * 5) % 9)


func _add_collectible(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i, kind: String, amount: int, spawn_guards: bool) -> void:
	var area := Area2D.new()
	area.name = "TreasureChest"
	area.position = _cell_center(cell)
	area.collision_layer = LAYER_TRIGGER
	area.collision_mask = LAYER_PLAYER
	area.set_meta("kind", kind)
	area.set_meta("amount", amount)
	area.set_meta("opened", false)
	parent.add_child(area)

	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.name = "ChestSprite"
	sprite.texture = _closed_chest_texture()
	area.add_child(sprite)

	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.name = "ChestBlocker"
	body.position = area.position
	body.collision_layer = LAYER_WORLD
	body.collision_mask = 0
	parent.add_child(body)

	var body_collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var body_shape := RectangleShape2D.new()
	body_shape.size = Vector2(24.0, 20.0)
	body_collision.shape = body_shape
	body.add_child(body_collision)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	collision.name = "ChestCollision"
	var shape := CircleShape2D.new()
	shape.radius = 22.0
	collision.shape = shape
	area.add_child(collision)
	area.body_entered.connect(_on_collectible_body_entered.bind(area))

	if spawn_guards:

+func _try_heal_player_from_fountain(_area: Area2D, show_full_message: bool) -> void:

+
+	var before := player_hp
+	player_hp = mini(PLAYER_MAX_HP, player_hp + FOUNTAIN_HEAL_AMOUNT)
+	var healed := player_hp - before

+	_show_player_heal_popup(healed)
+	_update_hud()
+	_update_stats_panel()

def draw_corridor_art(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, theme: dict, rooms: list[tuple[int, int, int, int]], connections: list[tuple[int, int]]) -> None:
    for a, b in connections:
        ax, ay = room_center(rooms[a])
        bx, by = room_center(rooms[b])
        points = [(ax * TILE + TILE // 2, ay * TILE + TILE // 2), (bx * TILE + TILE // 2, ay * TILE + TILE // 2), (bx * TILE + TILE // 2, by * TILE + TILE // 2)]
        for p1, p2 in zip(points, points[1:]):
            draw.line((*p1, *p2), fill=shade(theme["wall"], -18), width=TILE * 4)
            draw.line((*p1, *p2), fill=shade(theme["floor"], -10), width=TILE * 3)
            draw.line((*p1, *p2), fill=theme["floor_alt"], width=2)
            draw.line((p1[0], p1[1] + TILE, p2[0], p2[1] + TILE), fill=shade(theme["floor"], -34), width=3)


def draw_room_art(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, rng: random.Random, theme: dict, rooms: list[tuple[int, int, int, int]]) -> None:
    for index, room in enumerate(rooms):
        x, y, w, h = room
        px, py = x * TILE, y * TILE
        rw, rh = w * TILE, h * TILE
        shadow = (px - 14, py - 10, px + rw + 16, py + rh + 18)
        draw.rectangle(shadow, fill=shade(theme["wall"], -22))

func _add_player_animation(frames: SpriteFrames, name: String, row: int, columns: Array[int], speed: float, loops := true) -> void:
	_add_character_animation(frames, player_sheet, name, row, columns, speed, loops)


func _add_character_animation(frames: SpriteFrames, sheet: Texture2D, name: String, row: int, columns: Array[int], speed: float, loops := true) -> void:
	frames.add_animation(name)
	frames.set_animation_loop(name, loops)
	frames.set_animation_speed(name, speed)
	for column in columns:
		frames.add_frame(name, _character_frame_texture(sheet, column, row))


func _player_frame_texture(column: int, row: int) -> Texture2D:
	return _character_frame_texture(player_sheet, column, row)


func _character_frame_texture(sheet: Texture2D, column: int, row: int) -> Texture2D:
	var frame := AtlasTexture.new()
	frame.atlas = sheet
	frame.region = Rect2(
		column * PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		row * PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE
	)

+        draw.polygon([(x + 16, y + 8 + bob), (x + 8, y + 1 + bob), (x + 24, y + 1 + bob)], fill=fin)
+        eye = (x + 12, y + 16 + bob)
+        mouth = (x + 11, y + 22 + bob, x + 21, y + 26 + bob)
+    draw.ellipse((eye[0] - 2, eye[1] - 2, eye[0] + 2, eye[1] + 2), fill=(10, 21, 30, 255))
+    if direction in ("up", "down"):
+        draw.ellipse((eye[0] + 6, eye[1] - 2, eye[0] + 10, eye[1] + 2), fill=(10, 21, 30, 255))
+    if mode == "attack":
+        draw.arc(mouth, 0, 180, fill=(255, 245, 218, 255), width=2)
+        for i in range(2):
+            bx = x + 16 + (i * 6) - frame % 3
+            by = y + 4 + i * 6
+            draw.ellipse((bx - 2, by - 2, bx + 2, by + 2), outline=(175, 238, 255, 220), width=1)
+    elif mode == "death":
+        draw.line((x + 10, y + 14, x + 15, y + 19), fill=(20, 30, 38, 255), width=2)
+        draw.line((x + 15, y + 14, x + 10, y + 19), fill=(20, 30, 38, 255), width=2)
+
+
+def generate_enemy_sheet(level: int, color: tuple[int, int, int]) -> None:
+    image = Image.new("RGBA", (SHEET_W, SHEET_H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image, "RGBA")
+    row_directions = {0: "down", 1: "down", 2: "right", 3: "right", 4: "up", 5: "up", 6: "left", 7: "left"}
+    for row, direction in row_directions.items():
+        for col in range(24):
+            mode = "move"
+            if col >= 8:
+                mode = "attack"
+            if col >= 20:
+                mode = "death"
+            draw_enemy_frame(draw, col * FRAME, row * FRAME, color, direction, col, mode)
+    image.save(OUT_DIR / f"ocean_enemy_level{level:02d}.png")
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for index, layout in enumerate(LEVEL_LAYOUTS, start=1):
+        generate_map(index, layout, OCEAN_THEMES[index - 1])
+        generate_enemy_sheet(index, ENEMY_PALETTES[index - 1])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch

+func _build_air_bubble_sprite_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
+	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
+	frames.remove_animation("default")
+	frames.add_animation("bubble")
+	frames.set_animation_loop("bubble", true)
+	frames.set_animation_speed("bubble", 7.0)
+	for index in range(4):
+		frames.add_frame("bubble", _create_air_bubble_texture(index))
+	return frames
+
+
+func _create_air_bubble_texture(index: int) -> Texture2D:
+	var size := 32
+	var image := Image.create(size, size, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
+	image.fill(Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
+	var center := Vector2(size * 0.5, size * 0.5 + sin(float(index) * 1.4) * 0.8)
+	var radius := 12.0 + float(index % 2)
+	for y in range(size):
+		for x in range(size):
+			var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+			var distance := point.distance_to(center)
+			if distance <= radius:
+				var edge := clampf((radius - distance) / radius, 0.0, 1.0)
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.62, 0.95, 1.0, 0.12 + edge * 0.16))
+			if distance > radius - 1.8 and distance <= radius + 0.9:
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.82, 1.0, 1.0, 0.72))
+	var glint_offset := Vector2(-5.0 + float(index), -6.0)
+	for y in range(size):
+		for x in range(size):
+			var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+			if point.distance_to(center + glint_offset) <= 2.2:
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.9))
+	for i in range(2):
+		var bubble_center := center + Vector2(7.0 + i * 3.0, -10.0 - index - i * 4.0)
+		for y in range(size):
+			for x in range(size):
+				var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+				var distance := point.distance_to(bubble_center)
+				if distance <= 2.3:
+					image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.78, 1.0, 1.0, 0.62))
+	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)

+## 章节定位
+
+第三章从森林进入海洋场景，主题是“带着空气泡探索沉没遗迹”。角色进入海底后，头部始终显示循环的气泡护罩动画，表示角色依靠魔法气泡在水下行动。
+
+## 核心体验
+
+- 地图视觉：蓝绿色海底房间、珊瑚、海草、水纹、沉没遗迹走廊。
+- 怪物主题：礁猎者，一种在遗迹中巡游的海洋怪物。
+- 玩法节奏：继续沿用探索、宝箱、金币、宝石、出口推进的结构，但气氛从地面地牢转为水下遗迹。
+- 战斗表现：礁猎者拥有待机、移动、攻击、死亡动画。靠近角色后停下并播放咬击/喷泡攻击动作，再结算伤害。
+
+## 怪物设定
+
+名称：礁猎者
+
+行为：
+
+- 平时在宝箱附近巡游。
+- 随关卡提升，主动追击比例提高。
+- 靠近角色时播放方向性攻击动画。
+- 被击败时播放死亡动画。
+
+成长：
+
+- 第 1-8 关血量递增为 1-8。
+- 第 1-8 关攻击力递增为 1-8。
+- 每关使用不同颜色的礁猎者素材，形成层级变化。
+
+## 角色海洋状态
+
+角色在第三章中始终带有头部气泡动画：
+
+- 不影响移动、攻击、受击、倒下动画。
+- 气泡作为独立子动画挂在角色头部。
+- 视觉用途是强化“海洋章节”身份，并为后续氧气机制预留表现基础。
+
+## 后续可扩展
+
+- 氧气值：气泡可以从纯视觉升级为资源条。
+- 海流区域：改变角色移动方向或速度。
+- 珊瑚障碍：强化海底空间结构。
+- 珍珠宝箱：第三章可替换成海洋宝箱视觉。
+- 水下 Boss：第 8 关可加入大型深海怪物作为章节终点。
*** End Patch

        draw.rectangle(shadow, fill=shade(theme["wall"], -22))

func _add_player_animation(frames: SpriteFrames, name: String, row: int, columns: Array[int], speed: float, loops := true) -> void:
	_add_character_animation(frames, player_sheet, name, row, columns, speed, loops)


func _add_character_animation(frames: SpriteFrames, sheet: Texture2D, name: String, row: int, columns: Array[int], speed: float, loops := true) -> void:
	frames.add_animation(name)
	frames.set_animation_loop(name, loops)
	frames.set_animation_speed(name, speed)
	for column in columns:
		frames.add_frame(name, _character_frame_texture(sheet, column, row))


func _player_frame_texture(column: int, row: int) -> Texture2D:
	return _character_frame_texture(player_sheet, column, row)


func _character_frame_texture(sheet: Texture2D, column: int, row: int) -> Texture2D:
	var frame := AtlasTexture.new()
	frame.atlas = sheet
	frame.region = Rect2(
		column * PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		row * PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE,
		PLAYER_FRAME_SIZE
	)

+	player_bubble_anim.z_index = 8
+	player_bubble_anim.play()
+	player.add_child(player_bubble_anim)
+
+
+func _build_air_bubble_sprite_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
+	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
+	frames.remove_animation("default")
+	frames.add_animation("bubble")
+	frames.set_animation_loop("bubble", true)
+	frames.set_animation_speed("bubble", 7.0)
+	for index in range(4):
+		frames.add_frame("bubble", _create_air_bubble_texture(index))
+	return frames
+
+
+func _create_air_bubble_texture(index: int) -> Texture2D:
+	var size := 32
+	var image := Image.create(size, size, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
+	image.fill(Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
+	var center := Vector2(size * 0.5, size * 0.5 + sin(float(index) * 1.4) * 0.8)
+	var radius := 12.0 + float(index % 2)
+	for y in range(size):
+		for x in range(size):
+			var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+			var distance := point.distance_to(center)
+			if distance <= radius:
+				var edge := clampf((radius - distance) / radius, 0.0, 1.0)
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.62, 0.95, 1.0, 0.12 + edge * 0.16))
+			if distance > radius - 1.8 and distance <= radius + 0.9:
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.82, 1.0, 1.0, 0.72))
+	var glint_offset := Vector2(-5.0 + float(index), -6.0)
+	for y in range(size):
+		for x in range(size):
+			var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+			if point.distance_to(center + glint_offset) <= 2.2:
+				image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.9))
+	for i in range(2):
+		var bubble_center := center + Vector2(7.0 + i * 3.0, -10.0 - index - i * 4.0)
+		for y in range(size):
+			for x in range(size):
+				var point := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5)
+				var distance := point.distance_to(bubble_center)
+				if distance <= 2.3:
+					image.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.78, 1.0, 1.0, 0.62))
+	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)

+# 第三章：海底气泡遗迹
+
+## 章节定位
+
+第三章从森林进入海洋场景，主题是“带着空气泡探索沉没遗迹”。角色进入海底后，头部始终显示循环的气泡护罩动画，表示角色依靠魔法气泡在水下行动。
+
+## 核心体验
+
+- 地图视觉：蓝绿色海底房间、珊瑚、海草、水纹、沉没遗迹走廊。
+- 怪物主题：礁猎者，一种在遗迹中巡游的海洋怪物。
+- 玩法节奏：继续沿用探索、宝箱、金币、宝石、出口推进的结构，但气氛从地面地牢转为水下遗迹。
+- 战斗表现：礁猎者拥有待机、移动、攻击、死亡动画。靠近角色后停下并播放咬击/喷泡攻击动作，再结算伤害。
+
+## 怪物设定
+
+名称：礁猎者
+
+行为：
+
+- 平时在宝箱附近巡游。
+- 随关卡提升，主动追击比例提高。
+- 靠近角色时播放方向性攻击动画。
+- 被击败时播放死亡动画。
+
+成长：
+
+- 第 1-8 关血量递增为 1-8。
+- 第 1-8 关攻击力递增为 1-8。
+- 每关使用不同颜色的礁猎者素材，形成层级变化。
+
+## 角色海洋状态
+
+角色在第三章中始终带有头部气泡动画：
+
+- 不影响移动、攻击、受击、倒下动画。
+- 气泡作为独立子动画挂在角色头部。
+- 视觉用途是强化“海洋章节”身份，并为后续氧气机制预留表现基础。
+
+## 后续可扩展
+
+- 氧气值：气泡可以从纯视觉升级为资源条。
+- 海流区域：改变角色移动方向或速度。
+- 珊瑚障碍：强化海底空间结构。
+- 珍珠宝箱：第三章可替换成海洋宝箱视觉。
+- 水下 Boss：第 8 关可加入大型深海怪物作为章节终点。
*** End Patch

def generate_enemy_sheet(level: int, color: tuple[int, int, int]) -> None:
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (SHEET_W, SHEET_H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image, "RGBA")
    row_directions = {0: "down", 1: "down", 2: "right", 3: "right", 4: "up", 5: "up", 6: "left", 7: "left"}
    for row, direction in row_directions.items():
        for col in range(24):
            mode = "move"
            if col >= 8:
                mode = "attack"
            if col >= 20:
                mode = "death"
            draw_enemy_frame(draw, col * FRAME, row * FRAME, color, direction, col, mode)
    image.save(OUT_DIR / f"ocean_enemy_level{level:02d}.png")


def main() -> None:
    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for index, layout in enumerate(LEVEL_LAYOUTS, start=1):
        generate_map(index, layout, OCEAN_THEMES[index - 1])
        generate_enemy_sheet(index, ENEMY_PALETTES[index - 1])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

+# 第四章：熔岩炉心遗迹
+
+## 章节定位
+
+第四章从海底遗迹推进到地下火山区域，主题是“穿越熔岩炉心，寻找被火焰封存的宝藏”。场景视觉从蓝绿色海洋转为黑曜石、熔岩裂缝、余烬和高热石室。
+
+## 核心体验
+
+- 地图视觉：火山岩房间、熔岩裂纹、炉心大厅、炽热走廊。
+- 怪物主题：余烬灵，一种由火山魔力凝聚出的火焰怪物。
+- 玩法节奏：保持探索、宝箱、金币、宝石、出口推进的结构，但战斗压力比第三章更直接。
+- 战斗表现：余烬灵拥有待机、移动、攻击、死亡动画。靠近角色后停步并播放火焰扑击动画，再结算伤害。
+
+## 怪物设定
+
+名称：余烬灵
+
+行为：
+
+- 平时在宝箱附近漂浮巡逻。
+- 随关卡提升，主动追击比例提高。
+- 靠近角色时播放方向性火焰攻击动画。
+- 被击败时播放熄灭/崩散死亡动画。
+
+成长：
+
+- 第 1-8 关血量递增为 1-8。
+- 第 1-8 关攻击力递增为 1-8。
+- 每关使用不同颜色的余烬灵素材，表现从普通火焰到深红炉心的进阶。
+
+## 地图结构
+
+第四章继续使用 8 个关卡，每关保持 9 个房间结构，但视觉上改为火山遗迹：
+
+- 入口区：低温灰烬地面，适合让玩家熟悉新视觉。
+- 中段区：熔岩裂纹更密，余烬灵颜色更亮。
+- 后段区：黑曜石墙体和炉心光更强，敌人血量与攻击力更高。
+
+## 后续可扩展
+
+- 熔岩伤害区：踩到裂缝后持续掉血。
+- 冷却泉：与第二章泉水相反，作为火山章节的回复点。
+- 火焰抗性装备：降低余烬灵伤害。
+- 炉心 Boss：第 8 关加入大型火焰守卫作为章节终点。
*** End Patch

def generate_enemy_sheet(level: int, color: tuple[int, int, int]) -> None:
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (SHEET_W, SHEET_H), (0, 0, 0, 0))

    row_directions = {0: "down", 1: "down", 2: "right", 3: "right", 4: "up", 5: "up", 6: "left", 7: "left"}
    for row, direction in row_directions.items():
        for col in range(24):
            mode = "move"
            if col >= 8:
                mode = "attack"
            if col >= 20:
                mode = "death"
            draw_enemy_frame(draw, col * FRAME, row * FRAME, color, direction, col, mode)
    image.save(OUT_DIR / f"ocean_enemy_level{level:02d}.png")

+def generate_enemy_sheet(level: int, color: tuple[int, int, int]) -> None:
+    image = Image.new("RGBA", (SHEET_W, SHEET_H), (0, 0, 0, 0))

+            if col >= 8:

+            if col >= 20:
+                mode = "death"
+            draw_enemy_frame(draw, col * FRAME, row * FRAME, color, direction, col, mode)
+    image.save(OUT_DIR / f"volcano_enemy_level{level:02d}.png")
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for index, layout in enumerate(LEVEL_LAYOUTS, start=1):
+        generate_map(index, layout, VOLCANO_THEMES[index - 1])
+        generate_enemy_sheet(index, ENEMY_PALETTES[index - 1])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":

+# 第四章：熔岩炉心遗迹
+
+## 章节定位
+
+第四章从海底遗迹推进到地下火山区域，主题是“穿越熔岩炉心，寻找被火焰封存的宝藏”。场景视觉从蓝绿色海洋转为黑曜石、熔岩裂缝、余烬和高热石室。
+
+## 核心体验
+
+- 地图视觉：火山岩房间、熔岩裂纹、炉心大厅、炽热走廊。
+- 怪物主题：余烬灵，一种由火山魔力凝聚出的火焰怪物。
+- 玩法节奏：保持探索、宝箱、金币、宝石、出口推进的结构，但战斗压力比第三章更直接。
+- 战斗表现：余烬灵拥有待机、移动、攻击、死亡动画。靠近角色后停步并播放火焰扑击动画，再结算伤害。
+
+## 怪物设定
+
+名称：余烬灵
+
+行为：
+
+- 平时在宝箱附近漂浮巡逻。
+- 随关卡提升，主动追击比例提高。
+- 靠近角色时播放方向性火焰攻击动画。
+- 被击败时播放熄灭/崩散死亡动画。
+
+成长：
+
+- 第 1-8 关血量递增为 1-8。
+- 第 1-8 关攻击力递增为 1-8。
+- 每关使用不同颜色的余烬灵素材，表现从普通火焰到深红炉心的进阶。
+
+## 地图结构
+
+第四章继续使用 8 个关卡，每关保持 9 个房间结构，但视觉上改为火山遗迹：
+
+- 入口区：低温灰烬地面，适合让玩家熟悉新视觉。
+- 中段区：熔岩裂纹更密，余烬灵颜色更亮。
+- 后段区：黑曜石墙体和炉心光更强，敌人血量与攻击力更高。
+
+## 后续可扩展
+
+- 熔岩伤害区：踩到裂缝后持续掉血。
+- 冷却泉：与第二章泉水相反，作为火山章节的回复点。
+- 火焰抗性装备：降低余烬灵伤害。
+- 炉心 Boss：第 8 关加入大型火焰守卫作为章节终点。
*** End Patch

[328] tool exec_command call: {"cmd":"python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw\nroot = Path('/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-2d-treasure/launcher/QuietDungeonTreasure.app/Contents/Resources')\nroot.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nimg = Image.new('RGBA', (1024, 1024), (10, 18, 26, 255))\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img, 'RGBA')\nfor y in range(1024):\n    t = y / 1023\n    color = (int(12 + 42*t), int(26 + 38*t), int(42 + 35*t), 255)\n    d.line((0, y, 1024, y), fill=color)\n# dungeon tile base\nd.rounded_rectangle((132, 150, 892, 874), radius=90, fill=(34, 44, 50, 255), outline=(142, 226, 220, 255), width=18)\n# gem/treasure\nd.polygon([(512, 245), (706, 410), (626, 695), (512, 790), (398, 695), (318, 410)], fill=(51, 204, 229, 255), outline=(230, 255, 245, 255))\nd.polygon([(512, 245), (598, 423), (512, 790), (426, 423)], fill=(96, 245, 190, 210))\nd.line((318, 410, 706, 410), fill=(236, 255, 250, 230), width=14)\nd.line((512, 245, 512, 790), fill=(224, 255, 255, 190), width=10)\n# chest corners\nd.rounded_rectangle((235, 700, 392, 810), radius=18, fill=(139, 78, 35, 255), outline=(54, 33, 22, 255), width=8)\nd.rectangle((235, 700, 392, 745), fill=(238, 174, 67, 255), outline=(54, 33, 22, 255), width=6)\nd.rounded_rectangle((632, 700, 789, 810), radius=18, fill=(139, 78, 35, 255), outline=(54, 33, 22, 255), width=8)\nd.rectangle((632, 700, 789, 745), fill=(238, 174, 67, 255), outline=(54, 33, 22, 255), width=6)\n# chapter hints: water + lava\nd.arc((210, 170, 450, 300), 180, 350, fill=(96, 225, 255, 230), width=20)\nd.arc((574, 168, 814, 304), 190, 352, fill=(255, 116, 58, 230), width=20)\nimg.save(root/'AppIcon.png')\nPY","workdir":"/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-2d-treasure","yield_time_ms":1000,"max_output_tokens":12000}
